                    [32m'        self,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        url: str,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        headers: dict[str, str],\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        on_stdout: OutputHandler[str],\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        on_stderr: OutputHandler[str],\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ) -> None:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Open a sync httpx_ws WebSocket, demux stdout/stderr until EOF, then close.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        The surrounding method is ``async def`` (callable via ``asyncio.run`` from sync code)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        so user-supplied callbacks may be ``async def`` and need awaiting. The httpx_ws session\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        itself is sync — its blocking ``receive()`` is bridged onto a worker thread inside\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m"        :func:`std_demux_stream_httpx_ws` so we don't stall the event loop. The WS upgrade\r\n"[39m +
                    [32m"        reuses ``self._http_client``'s connection pool, sharing TLS context + DNS cache with\r\n"[39m +
                    [32m'        every other sync HTTP request the SDK makes.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        with httpx_ws.connect_ws(url, self._http_client, headers=headers) as ws:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            await std_demux_stream_httpx_ws(ws, on_stdout, on_stderr)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @intercept_errors(message_prefix="Failed to execute command: ")\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @with_instrumentation()\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def exec(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        command: str,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        cwd: str | None = None,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        env: dict[str, str] | None = None,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        timeout: int | None = None,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    ) -> ExecuteResponse:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """Execute a shell command in the Sandbox.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Args:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            command (str): Shell command to execute.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            cwd (str | None): Working directory for command execution. If not\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                specified, uses the sandbox working directory.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            env (dict[str, str] | None): Environment variables to set for the command.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            timeout (int | None): Maximum time in seconds to wait for the command\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                to complete.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Returns:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ExecuteResponse: Command execution results containing:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m"                - exit_code: The command's exit status\r\n"[39m +
                    [32m'                - result: Standard output from the command\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                - artifacts: ExecutionArtifacts object containing `stdout` (same as result)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                and `charts` (matplotlib charts metadata)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Example:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ```python\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # Simple command\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m`            response = sandbox.process.exec("echo 'Hello'")\r\n`[39m +
                    [32m'            print(response.artifacts.stdout)  # Prints: Hello\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'            # Command with working directory\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            result = sandbox.process.exec("ls", cwd="workspace/src")\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # Command with timeout\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            result = sandbox.process.exec("sleep 10", timeout=5)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ```\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        execute_request = ExecuteRequest(command=command, cwd=cwd, timeout=timeout, envs=env)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        response = self._api_client.execute_command(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            request=execute_request,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            _request_timeout=http_timeout(timeout + 5 if timeout else None),\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        )\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        result = response.result or ""\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        artifacts = ExecutionArtifacts(stdout=result, charts=[])\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return ExecuteResponse.model_construct(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            exit_code=(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                response.exit_code if response.exit_code is not None else response.additional_properties.get("code")\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ),\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            result=result,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            artifacts=artifacts,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            additional_properties=response.additional_properties,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        )\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @with_instrumentation()\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def code_run(\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'            additional_properties=response.additional_properties,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        )\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @intercept_errors(message_prefix="Failed to create session: ")\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @with_instrumentation()\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        """Open a sync httpx_ws WebSocket, demux stdout/stderr until EOF, then close.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        The surrounding method is ``async def`` (callable via ``asyncio.run`` from sync code)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        so user-supplied callbacks may be ``async def`` and need awaiting. The httpx_ws session\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        itself is sync — its blocking ``receive()`` is bridged onto a worker thread inside\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m"        :func:`std_demux_stream_httpx_ws` so we don't stall the event loop. The WS upgrade\r\n"[39m +

                    [32m'        every other sync HTTP request the SDK makes.\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'        with httpx_ws.connect_ws(url, self._http_client, headers=headers) as ws:\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'                to complete.\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Returns:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ExecuteResponse: Command execution results containing:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m"                - exit_code: The command's exit status\r\n"[39m +
                    [32m'                - result: Standard output from the command\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                - artifacts: ExecutionArtifacts object containing `stdout` (same as result)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                and `charts` (matplotlib charts metadata)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        Example:\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ```python\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # Simple command\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m`            response = sandbox.process.exec("echo 'Hello'")\r\n`[39m +
                    [32m'            print(response.artifacts.stdout)  # Prints: Hello\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # Command with working directory\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            result = sandbox.process.exec("ls", cwd="workspace/src")\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            # Command with timeout\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            result = sandbox.process.exec("sleep 10", timeout=5)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ```\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        """\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        execute_request = ExecuteRequest(command=command, cwd=cwd, timeout=timeout, envs=env)\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        response = self._api_client.execute_command(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            request=execute_request,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            _request_timeout=http_timeout(timeout + 5 if timeout else None),\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        )\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        result = response.result or ""\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        artifacts = ExecutionArtifacts(stdout=result, charts=[])\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        return ExecuteResponse.model_construct(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            exit_code=(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'                response.exit_code if response.exit_code is not None else response.additional_properties.get("code")\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            ),\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            result=result,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            artifacts=artifacts,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'            additional_properties=response.additional_properties,\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        )\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    @with_instrumentation()\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'    def code_run(\r\n'[39m +
                    [32m'        self,\r\n'[39m +

                    [32m'                - result: Standard output from the code\r\n'[39m +

Instead, after hook failure, fix the issue, re-stage, and create a NEW commit\n'[39m +
            [32m'- When staging files, prefer adding specific files by name rather than using "git add -A" or "git add .", which can accidentally include sensitive files (.env, credentials) or large binaries\n'[39m +
